# AUTHORED EXTENSIONS LEDGER — Phase 12 (Consistency Sweep)

**Created:** 2026-04-26 during Phase 12 Gate re-issue.
**Purpose:** Single sign-off register for every Authored Extension introduced during the v7.0.0 integration that requires human ratification before release.
**Owner of ledger:** Founder (or delegate). Owners of individual rows: see `Sign-off owner` column.
**Release-gate policy.** v7.0.0 release is **not gated** on every Authored Extension being `approved`. v7.0.0 **IS gated** on:
- All Phase-12.3 Security-or-Legal entries (AE-12.3-04, AE-12.3-05, AE-12.3-06) being `approved` or `acknowledged`;
- All Phase-12.4 Engineering+Finance entries (AE-12.4-01, AE-12.4-02) being `approved` or `acknowledged`;
- The Enterprise SLA Breaking Change (BC-12.4-01) being `acknowledged` by Sales/Legal.

Every other entry is non-blocking for v7.0.0 release but MUST be ratified before v7.1.0 cycle close.

**Compliance-history amendment (D-AE-013 closure; v7.2.0-REM Phase 7; 2026-05-20).** The four bullet-list policy clauses above are preserved as the authored-2026-04-26 prescriptive policy. They were *not* enforced at v7.0.0 stamp (2026-04-26), at v7.1.0 stamp (2026-04-28), or at v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp (2026-05-20) for the six release-gating rows named in the bullets. The actual rule applied across all three stamp events was a de-facto "acknowledged-by-default" posture; the bypass was first surfaced by the Phase AE audit on 2026-05-12 (D-AE-013 P1). The six release-gating rows were ratified at v7.2.0-REM Phase 7 AE Ratification Sweep Wave 1 on 2026-05-20 (5 approved + 1 acknowledged; AE-12.4-02 and BC-12.4-01 carry conditional v7.1.1 deliverables). The release-gate policy is tightened for the v7.1.1 stamp gate and all subsequent stamps via a new `release_gate_policy_compliance` CI gate (release-orchestration cluster; runtime artifact `tools/release/release_gate_policy_compliance.ts`; M02.3 implementation pack; runtime-active at v7.1.1 stamp per `AE-V72REM-08` 174-gate migration plan). The CI gate fails closed when any `pending` row whose `Target version` ≤ the stamp version is present; override path is `not_permitted_release_gate_integrity` (override structurally rejected). Full forensic record: `## Owner Notification — v7.1.0 program → ### Amendment — Actual Rule Applied at v7.1.0 Stamp (D-AE-013 Closure; v7.2.0-REM Phase 7 AE Ratification Sweep Wave 1; 2026-05-20)` below.

---

## Status Values

| Value | Meaning |
| :---- | :---- |
| `pending` | Authored, sign-off owner notified, no decision recorded |
| `approved` | Owner has explicitly ratified the extension |
| `rejected` | Owner has explicitly rejected; the extension MUST be reverted |
| `acknowledged` | Owner has read and accepts as-is for v7.0.0; revisit in v7.1.0 |
| `superseded` | A later extension supplants this one; cross-reference required |

## Owner Notification — v7.1.0 program

The v7.1.0 entries above were authored 2026-04-28 during the Phase 14.19 fix-pass (closed 2026-04-28; reconciliation entry `_integration/RECONCILIATION.md → Phase 14.19 fix-pass / Phase 14.0.1 / 14.9.1 / 14.10.1 / 14.14 / 14.18.1`). Initial owner notifications go out via the standard `RECONCILIATION.md` review process. Status updates should be applied directly to this file with the owner's name + date when status moves out of `pending`. Phase 14.20 closeout audits this section as a hard gate; v7.1.0 stamp is blocked on every entry being either `approved`, `acknowledged`, or `superseded` (no `pending` rows remain).

### Amendment — Actual Rule Applied at v7.1.0 Stamp (D-AE-013 Closure; v7.2.0-REM Phase 7 AE Ratification Sweep Wave 1; 2026-05-20)

**The paragraph above is preserved as the authored-2026-04-28 prescriptive policy. It is NOT a description of what actually happened at v7.1.0 stamp.** Per `_audit/DEFECT_LEDGER.md → Phase AE → D-AE-013 (P1)`, the prescriptive "no `pending` rows remain" hard-gate claim was *not* enforced when Master Spec v7.1.0 stamped on 2026-04-28: approximately 49 rows across the v7.1.0 program cluster (Phase 14.4 / 14.5 / 14.6 / 14.7 / 14.8 / 14.9 / 14.10 / 14.14 / 14.18.1 / 14.0.1 sub-clusters) remained `pending` at stamp time, including the six rows in this section (`AE-12.3-04`, `AE-12.3-05`, `AE-12.3-06`, `AE-12.4-01`, `AE-12.4-02`, `BC-12.4-01`) flagged "RELEASE-GATING for v7.0.0" since the 2026-04-26 Phase 12 Gate re-issue. The v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp on 2026-05-20 inherited the same bypass without remediation for these six legacy rows; v7.1.0a closed only the v7.2.0-REM Phase 1–6 P0 dependency surface.

**Actual rule applied at v7.0.0 stamp (2026-04-26), v7.1.0 stamp (2026-04-28), and v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp (2026-05-20).** Stamp execution proceeded under a de-facto "acknowledged-by-default" policy: rows with `pending` status were neither explicitly approved nor explicitly acknowledged, but the stamp committee treated unblocked-on-build rows as `non-objection by absence of dissent` and proceeded. The bypass spanned three stamp events without being surfaced as a halt condition. The drift was first surfaced by the Phase AE audit on 2026-05-12 (`_audit/PHASE_AE_FINDINGS.md`) and filed as D-AE-013 (P1; considered for P0 escalation but held at P1 because the drift is recoverable via preamble amendment without a code-path defect). The forward-fix AE row `AE-V72REM-07` was authored on 2026-05-15 at v7.2.0-REM Phase 0 kickoff and ratified on 2026-05-20 at the v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp under Founder sole-signer posture per `_audit/PRODUCTION_READINESS_VERDICT.md §9.1` + `AE-V72REM-00`.

**Why the bypass occurred.** Three contributing root causes, per `_audit/PHASE_AE_FINDINGS.md §10` + `_audit/AE_RATIFICATION_RECOMMENDATIONS.md §5 Question 1`:

1. **No CI gate enforcement.** The "hard gate" was prose-only. No `release_gate_policy_compliance` CI gate ran at stamp time to mechanically block the stamp on `pending` rows in scope. The gate is authored in this amendment (see "v7.1.1 stamp tightening commitment" below).
2. **Sole-signer staffing posture pre-dated the formal governance contract.** All 5 verdict sign-off slots (Tech Lead, Pricing Owner, Security Officer, Compliance Officer, GTM Lead) were unfilled at v7.0.0 / v7.1.0 stamp times; the formal sole-signer posture (`AE-V72REM-00`) was not registered until v7.2.0-REM Phase 0 (2026-05-15). The named-role owners listed in this section's `Sign-off Owner` column (Security + Legal; Sales + Legal + Comms; Engineering + Finance; Founder + GTM Lead) had no human counterpart who could affirmatively ratify within the stamp window.
3. **Operational urgency.** v7.0.0 stamp closed the v6.0.0 → v7.0.0 integration program; v7.1.0 stamp closed the Surface-Abstraction & Dual-Maya program. Stamp deferral pending ratification would have indefinitely blocked downstream Linear cycles. The stamp committee's implicit cost-benefit was "stamp now; ratify in-stride." That implicit decision is now made explicit in this amendment and bound to a hard remediation surface at v7.1.1.

**v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp posture (2026-05-20).** Per `AE-V72REM-07` ratification at the v7.1.0a stamp + the `## v7.2.0-REM Program — Authored Extensions (Phase 0 → Phase 20) — 2026-05-15` → `**Release-gate policy (v7.2.0-REM cadence).**` preamble in the v7.2.0-REM Authored Extensions block below, the v7.1.0a stamp's release-gate posture is documented as: the seven v7.2.0-REM P0 closure dependencies (`AE-V72REM-01 / -02 / -03 / -04 / -05 / -06 / -09`) ratified before stamp; the legacy v7.0.0 release-gate dependencies (this section's 6 rows) were inherited *unratified* into v7.1.0a and ratified at v7.2.0-REM Phase 7 (this amendment block + the 6 row status transitions in the Phase 12.3 / 12.4 tables above).

**v7.1.1 stamp tightening commitment.** The v7.1.1 stamp gate enforces the documented policy without exception. Specifically:

1. **Zero `pending` rows in the v7.1.0-program residual queue** (the 7 rows enumerated in `CLAUDE.md §16` v7.1.0a stamp-state bullet — `AE-14.9-01`, `AE-14.10-07`, `AE-14.14-21`, `AE-14.18.1-01`, `AE-14.18.1-02`, `AE-14.0.1-01`, `AE-14.0.1-02`) before v7.1.1 stamps. (`AE-14.10-07` already `approved 2026-05-19` per v7.2.0-REM Phase 4.4.)
2. **Zero `pending` rows in the v7.1.1 stamp-gate inheritance set** (~140 rows across Phase 1V / 2V / 3V / 3V+ / 6R / V7 / V8.4 / V9 / 10V / V11 / V12 / V13 / Phase 14.x cluster + the residual Phase 12.x DEF cluster + the v7.2.0-REM Phase 1–6 AE rows that have not yet ratified — see `_audit/AE_RATIFICATION_RECOMMENDATIONS.md §2` per-cluster enumeration and `§3` ratification sequence waves 1–11).
3. **Release-gate-policy CI gate authoring.** A new CI gate `release_gate_policy_compliance` (release-orchestration cluster; scheduled into M02.3 implementation pack per `AE-V72REM-07` clause iii) blocks any stamp where the `_integration/AUTHORED_EXTENSIONS_LEDGER.md` walker finds a `pending` row whose `Target version` ≤ the stamp version. Gate runtime artifact target: `tools/release/release_gate_policy_compliance.ts` (M02.3 pack; runtime-active at v7.1.1 stamp per `AE-V72REM-08` 174-gate migration plan). Override path: `not_permitted_release_gate_integrity`; rationale floor: not applicable (override is rejected).
4. **No silent debt.** Any future stamp-time deferral of a `pending` row requires either (a) explicit owner ratification with named-role sign-off (or AE-V72REM-00 sole-signer posture invocation), (b) explicit row supersession with forward-link, or (c) explicit row re-targeting to a later stamp version recorded in a dedicated `target_ratification_version` ledger column (the column upgrade is itself part of the v7.1.1 hygiene pass per `D-AE-001`).
5. **v7.2.0 stamp final-disposition gate.** Per `AE-V72REM-08`, the v7.2.0 stamp gate re-runs `release_gate_policy_compliance` against the entire ledger (every section, every row) as a final ship-readiness check. The ship-ready stamp (v7.2.0) cannot land with any silent `pending` row inheritance debt.

**v7.2.0-REM Phase 7 closure (this amendment).** Six release-gating rows ratified per the row-level status transitions in the Phase 12.3 / 12.4 tables above: `AE-12.3-04` → `approved`; `AE-12.3-05` → `approved`; `AE-12.3-06` → `approved`; `AE-12.4-01` → `approved`; `AE-12.4-02` → `approved` (originally conditional on the v7.1.1 §34.18.3 Solo-row authoring pass per `D-AS-004` / `D-AS-005`; condition satisfied 2026-06-21 by `_audit/PHASE_V72REM_YEAR1_PLAN_MIX_P1_VERIFY.md`); `BC-12.4-01` → `acknowledged` (Legal sign-off on customer-notification protocol owed before pre-v7.0.0 Enterprise contract amendment campaign launches). D-AE-013 transitions `open → remediated 2026-05-20` per the canonical-row update in `_audit/DEFECT_LEDGER.md`. Reconciliation log entry at `_integration/RECONCILIATION.md → v7.2.0-REM Program → Phase 7 — AE Ratification Sweep Wave 1 — Phase 12.3 / 12.4 Release-Gating Closure (2026-05-20)`.

**Sign-off authority for this amendment.** Founder sole-signer posture per `AE-V72REM-00` + `_audit/PRODUCTION_READINESS_VERDICT.md §9.1` (Blake Henry Rowley, 2026-05-20). Counsel sign-off on `BC-12.4-01` customer-notification protocol pending; trigger fires 5 BD after Legal Counsel is engaged. Engineering Director sign-off on the AE bundle: Blake Henry Rowley sole-signer posture per `AE-V72REM-00`; named-role counter-signature trigger active within 5 BD of each named-role hire (Tech Lead, Pricing Owner, Security Officer, Compliance Officer, GTM Lead per `Verdict §9.1`).

## Phase 7 V7 Audit Remediation Authored Extensions (2026-05-07)

**Stack-alignment confirmation.** All 7 AE entries align to the chosen stack: **Convex** (cascade walker mutation-layer + same-region replica deployment configuration + snapshot residency partitioning + serializable-transaction idempotency markers); **AWS S3** (residency-bound replication policies + backup blob residency partitioning + per-region snapshot catalogs); **Anthropic** (`claude-haiku-4-5` powering the §48.4.4 PII detector for Bridge Event body text sweep — extends existing content-validators pipeline); **PagerDuty** (Ops paging on `dsar.cascade.partial_failure` SEV-2 to `dsar-ops` queue + SEV-1 escalation to `dsar-ops-escalation` queue); **Loops.so** (subject-facing DSAR resume / extension / administrative-closure email notifications via existing compliance-critical retry curve); **Datadog** (synthetic chaos test for `dns_failover_target_residency_match`; SLA breach alarms; sub-threshold PII finding metrics); **Stripe** (no new billing surface — but `legal_entity` Stripe Customer model preserved per existing §34.10.5 contract); **WorkOS** (no new auth surface; DSAR cascade walker honors per-region User-row residency anchoring); **PostHog** (cascade-pack and DR-pack events mirrored per Appendix C → Appendix G coverage CI gate); **Terraform / IaC** (S3 replication policy validation hook + cross-region apply-time rejection); **GitHub Actions** (CI gate runtime wiring per M02.3 / M11.3 / M21.3 implementation packs). No new third-party dependencies introduced.

**Release-gate policy.** Per the v7.1.0 release-gate policy at the top of this ledger, all 7 V9 `pending` rows MUST ratify before v7.1.1 stamps. Ratification queue: AE-V9-001 / AE-V9-002 / AE-V9-003 / AE-V9-004 (Privacy Officer + Engineering Director — DSAR cascade pack; outside counsel sign-off on AE-V9-004 statutory-interpretation choice) → AE-V9-005 (Privacy Officer + Engineering Director + Finance + Counsel — retention authoritative-home pack) → AE-V9-006 / AE-V9-007 (Engineering Director + Ops + Security + DPO + outside counsel — residency-bound DR pack). Privacy Officer + outside counsel are required signers on the V9 cluster because the contract anchors GDPR Art. 17 (right-to-erasure cross-firewall residue), Art. 12(3) (statutory fulfillment ceiling), and Chapter V (third-country transfer) decisions.

**V9 sign-off transition.** Pre-V9: V9 sign-off FAIL (6 open P0 in `dsar` / `residency` / `firewall_leakage` / `abuse_path` classes). **Stack-alignment confirmation for the V11 program.** The remediation pass aligns to the chosen Sourcera stack as follows: **GitHub Actions** (the `appendix_m_coverage_on_diff` gate runs in `.github/workflows/spec-lint.yml`; the GitHub App identity `@sourcera-spec-bot[bot]` posts inline review comments and writes the auto-row PR-branch commit; branch-protection required-check posture mirrors the existing Phase V8.4 spec-lint gate cluster — no new GitHub App required); **Convex** (cron `appendix_m_gate_nightly_digest` runs on the existing Convex cron infrastructure per §43.6; `AuditEvent` writes follow the existing §4.6.1 emitter; the new `SpecLintDigest` table is org-scoped to the Sourcera-internal `meta_org` per §50.2 Ops separation architecture); **Slack** (`#spec-ops` channel via the existing Sourcera Slack integration per §43.4.2; bot identity `@sourcera-spec-bot` is the same GitHub App account); **Loops.so** (fallback DL `lo_spec_ops_digest` via the existing `@spec-ops-digest` list — no new Loops.so List authoring required); **PagerDuty** (reviewer-rotation schedule `spec-ops-oncall-rotation` already exists at the v7.1.0 stamp; no new schedule); **Datadog** (service tag `spec-lint` + monitor `monitor:appendix_m_gate_nightly_digest_failed` registered per the §42.6 ops observability convention); **PostHog** (event `spec_lint.appendix_m_gate_run` registered in Appendix G); **Stripe** (no new Stripe surface — the `solo_tier_numeric_single_source` V11 lexical-pattern tightening prevents Solo subscription / per-eval / per-bid price-string drift that would otherwise mis-meter Stripe Billing Meter Events); **WorkOS** (no new WorkOS surface — the AE-3V-002 enumeration correction to 7 gates is pure documentation hygiene); **AWS S3** (the `s3_replication_policy_residency_bound` V11 coupled-override clarification names Terraform IaC apply-hook semantics per the existing §42.4.2 deployment-validator pattern); **Anthropic** (no model surface). No new third-party dependencies introduced. Runtime wiring for the 19 V11-new gates is owned per §M.5.5 assignment (M02.3 + M11.3 + M24.3 + release-orchestration pipeline `tools/release/stamp_gate.ts`).

### Stack-Alignment Summary for v7.2.0-REM (Phase 0)

All v7.2.0-REM Phase 0 scaffolding aligns to the chosen Sourcera stack — same stack alignment as the V11 + V12 + V13 programs. Phase 0 introduces no new third-party dependencies. Per-AE stack-alignment notes are owed at each AE's phase-close authoring pass; the phase-level notes are:

- **GitHub Actions** — v7.2.0-REM Phase 0 produces no new CI gate runtime wiring; Phase 1–6 produce ~6 new gates wired via existing `.github/workflows/spec-lint.yml` (M02.3) + release-orchestration. Phase 13 closes the 174-gate runtime-active migration.
- **Convex** — Phase 0 produces no new Convex entities. AE-V72REM-03 / -04 / -05 (Phase 4) extend §21.4 `CapabilityRegistryEntry` Convex table; AE-V72REM-06 (Phase 5) binds `legal_entity` enum to Stripe Customer creation Convex mutation.
- **Stripe** — AE-V72REM-06 binds Stripe Customer creation to the canonical `legal_entity` enum; AE-V72REM-05 affects Stripe meter-event routing for `first_pass_rfp_draft`.
- **WorkOS** — No new auth surface in v7.2.0-REM scaffolding.
- **Anthropic** — No new model surface in v7.2.0-REM scaffolding. (Phase 14 M.1 backfill is engine-concept registration, not new model wiring.)
- **PostHog / Datadog / PagerDuty / Loops.so / Sentry / Statuspage / Notion / AWS S3 / Vercel** — All v7.2.0-REM phases consume existing stack surfaces. Phase 13 (CI gate runtime wiring) is the heaviest wiring pass — Datadog monitors + PagerDuty rotations + nightly digests per the 174-gate scope.
- **No new third-party dependencies introduced.**

### Phase 1 Closure Note — D-2.2-042 (2026-05-15)

**Spec edits landed for AE-V72REM-01 (HeatMapCell vendor-opt-out sentinel admission closure):**

1. **§4.4.16 HeatMapCell Authoring Intent** — rewritten to declare the entity vendor-identity-free; the prior v7.1.0 sentinel-admission paragraph (`scope_kind=not_applicable` record in the registry) is retired with a marked D-2.2-042 remediation block explaining the enum-bound erosion gateway and the three structural grounds for retirement.
2. **§4.4.16 Vendor-Identity-Free Aggregate Invariant** — newly authored 4-predicate invariant: (a) every public render path strips vendor identifiers, (b) no Seller-FK column, (c) k-anonymity floor ≥ 5, (d) §4.5.6 vendor-identity-free surface-list membership. HeatMapCell satisfies all four.
3. **§4.4.16 field-table** — `vendor_opt_out_honored_at` row removed; `publication_status` Notes cell amended to declare `suppressed_by_opt_out` unreachable on this entity per the Invariant (shared-enum registration unchanged for the seven sibling entities that DO probe the Opt-Out Registry).
4. **§4.4.16 Failure Modes** — Failure Mode #1 reworded to reflect upstream-only opt-out propagation via §4.4.18 SellerSignal cohort-shrinkage (no direct HeatMapCell probe); new Failure Mode #4 added for the D-2.2-042 residual probe attempt closure (write-time validator + §M.5.13 CI gate).
5. **§4.4.16 Acceptance Criteria** — new AC #4 (write-time validator rejects vendor-identifying field with `heat_map_cell_vendor_identity_field_forbidden` HTTP 422; bound to `heat_map_cell_field_allowlist_drift_detect` CI gate); new AC #5 (§4.4.8 `vendor_opt_out_scope_kind` enum closed-set invariant + `enum_bound_no_inline_sentinel_admission` CI gate binding).
6. **§M13 HeatMapAggregationCard (sibling D-V72REM-PH1-001 closure)** — field-table row `vendor_opt_out_honored_at` removed; new prose paragraph authored declaring the entity inherits the §4.4.16 Vendor-Identity-Free Aggregate Invariant via the included-cells chain (cards aggregate cells which are vendor-identity-free; editorial narrative is content-validator-constrained against vendor-identifying copy).
7. **§4.4.8 Vendor Opt-Out Record consumer-redaction table** — single combined "MarketIntelligenceReport, HeatMapCell" row split into two rows: MarketIntelligenceReport retains its prior render-time live registry probe contract (§27.10.3 row 4); HeatMapCell receives a new row declaring upstream-only enforcement via §4.4.18 SellerSignal cohort-exclusion (§27.10.3 row 5) per the Invariant.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — in-place supersession exception clause.** The §21.4.1.B in-place supersession rule (Row 2 only — seller-side sibling preserves the v6.0.0 `id` UUID and `outcome_contract_id` FK; the visible `capability_id` string changes from `qa_suggestion` → `qa_suggestion_seller` while the storage-layer identifier is unchanged) is an explicit AC #1 exception clause that requires Pricing Owner + Engineering Lead dual-signoff because §4.8.2 AC #1 ("`capability_id` MUST be immutable once any AIOperation has referenced it") admits no general-purpose exception path in the v7.1.0 spec text. The exception is reasoned against the AC #1 spirit (the storage-layer `id` UUID — the true row-identity carrier under the Convex schema — is preserved; only the human-readable `capability_id` string changes, and the legacy string survives as a `CapabilityRegistryEntry.aliases` array element). The `aliases`-preservation interpretation is the narrowest possible exception that closes D-EM-001 without breaking the broader §4.8.2 immutability contract. AE-V72REM-PH4-01 binds the interpretation; the v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp Pricing Owner + Engineering Lead dual-signoff is the gate that ratifies it. Without ratification, the §21.4.1.B in-place supersession does NOT take effect at runtime — the v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp gate audit blocks the stamp.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — cost_multiplier per-row overrides.** Six of the nine newly-authored customer-billed rows (all six in §21.4.1.C plus the two `kb_suggestion_*` siblings in §21.4.1.D) carry per-row `cost_multiplier` values that diverge from the §4.8.2-default `1.050`: §21.4.1.C uses `1.250` (Sonnet/Haiku rows) and `1.333` (Opus row `deep_comparison`); §21.4.1.D uses `1.333` (Haiku rows). The divergence is driven by the §34.3.4 illustrative-rate-card published prices (which were authored against an implicit per-tier convention that did not match the §4.8.2 default). The per-row override is the narrowest possible interpretation of §4.8.2 that preserves the §34.3.4 published prices at v7.0.0 stamp. AE-V72REM-PH4.2-01 binds the per-row override pattern; if Pricing Owner sign-off rejects, the alternative is to (a) reconcile §34.3.4 prices to the formula-derived values from `(10.000, 1.050)` (lowers published prices, customer-facing rate-card change requires §4.8.9 30-day notice) OR (b) author a new `default_cost_multiplier_per_model_tier` registry feature (per-Sonnet/Haiku/Opus default multipliers) and migrate all `customer_billed` rows. Either alternative is a larger-scope change than per-row overrides; the per-row override pattern is the v7.1.0a stamp-minimum-blast-radius option.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — per-row multiplier overrides aligning to §34.14.1 published prices.** The §21.4.1.E row carries `value_multiplier=7.895` and `cost_multiplier=1.053` to preserve the §34.14.1 row 1 published prices (`value_price=$0.15`, `cost_price=$0.02`) at the published `cost_base=$0.019`. The default §4.8.2 multipliers `(10.000, 1.050)` would yield `value_price=$0.19` and `cost_price=$0.02`, which would lower-bound the formula above the published `$0.15`. Two alternative resolutions were considered and rejected: (a) restating `cost_base` from `$0.019` to `$0.015` so the default `× 10` multiplier yields the published `$0.15` exactly — REJECTED because `cost_base` is the published telemetry-derived value from §34.3.2 nightly recalc and changing it would be a §34.3.3 BC-change requiring 30-day customer notification; (b) restating the §34.14.1 published `value_price` from `$0.15` to `$0.19` to match the default-multiplier formula — REJECTED because it would constitute a customer-facing rate-card change requiring §4.8.9 30-day notice. The per-row override is the v7.1.0a-stamp-minimum-blast-radius option. Pricing Owner sign-off is required.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — independent OutcomeContract rows for `first_pass_rfp_draft` and `first_pass_responses`.** Per the §22.10.1.A row 1 entry, the OutcomeContract signal name `first_pass_rfp_draft` is shared by the batch capability `first_pass_responses` AND the new per-requirement capability `first_pass_rfp_draft`. §4.8.4 AC #1 ("Every OutcomeContract row MUST be referenced by exactly one CapabilityRegistryEntry row") REQUIRES the two capabilities to FK into **independent OutcomeContract rows** even though both rows happen to carry the same signal-name semantic. The shared-signal-name with independent-contracts pattern is permissible because the SellerOutcomeSignalConfig (§4.8.13) is indexed by `capability_id`, not by signal name; the cross-validator `first_pass_rfp_draft_outcome_contract_isolation` (§M.5.16) asserts a contract-revision on one MUST NOT retroactively re-resolve in-flight operations on the other (mirrors §4.8.4 acceptance criterion #2 and the §21.4.1.B / §21.4.1.D precedents). Engineering Lead sign-off is required.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — `first_pass_rfp_response_generator` alias resolution defers prose-hygiene rewrite to D-NOM-016.** The §44.6.3 / §4.8.2 AC #8 / §44.6.5 narrative-copy references to `first_pass_rfp_response_generator` are the non-canonical alias surfaced in this closure. The new §21.4.1.E row's `aliases` array carries the string; alias-rewrite at the AI invocation handler resolves it to `first_pass_rfp_draft` at runtime. The corpus-wide prose hygiene rewrite of the citing sections to use the canonical form is deferred to D-NOM-016 P2 follow-on closure (registered in this same closure batch's defect-ledger update; scope is cosmetic — the alias-rewrite mechanism already preserves runtime correctness; the prose rewrite is for hygiene of the §22.10.1.A Canonical-ID Primacy rule). The v7.2.0-REM Phase 4.3 scope is the registry-row authoring + §34 binding, not corpus-wide prose hygiene.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — family-root binding for `first_pass_*` deferred to D-EM-021.** A future closure may elect to register a `first_pass_family` rollup root binding both `first_pass_rfp_draft` (per-requirement) and `first_pass_responses` (batch) under a shared `capability_family` key for billing-ledger rollup display purposes — mirroring the §21.4.1.B `qa_suggestion_family` pattern or the §21.4.1.D `kb_suggestion_family` pattern. That decision is deferred to D-EM-021 P2 follow-on closure (registered in this same closure batch's defect-ledger update) with explicit Pricing Owner adjudication; if elected, the family-root would carry §21.4.1.B / §21.4.1.D-pattern semantics including the §4.8.2 AC #9 family-cardinality validator extension to enumerate `first_pass_family`. At v7.1.0a stamp the two rows are independent standalone capabilities; the §4.8.2 AC #9 closed-set lock holds at three families (`qa_suggestion`, `kb_suggestion`, `org_intelligence`) per the Phase 4.2 generalization.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — Free Allowance citation drift resolution defers to D-PXC-007.** §34.8.5 line 30239 states `free_allowance_ops = 10` for `first_pass_rfp_draft`; §34.14.1 row 1's Plan Gating column states "Free = 25 lifetime (§34.1.2)"; §34.1.2 row "First-Pass RFP Response Generator" L29670 does NOT state any per-capability lifetime free count (only "Wallet (Free $5 hard cap)"). The §21.4.1.E row uses `free_allowance_quantity_default = 10` honoring §34.8.5 as the runtime contract per the §34.8.5 narrative "This matrix is the runtime contract for every plan-gated capability." The §34.14.1 / §34.1.2 reconciliation is registered as D-PXC-007 P2 follow-on closure with Pricing Owner adjudication; either §34.1.2 is amended to state the explicit lifetime count OR §34.14.1 row 1's plan-gating column is amended to align with §34.8.5's 10. Engineering uses §34.8.5's value at runtime in the interim.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — closed-set integrity invariant precedent.** The §21.4.6 closed-set membership lock (mutation requires Ops Finance + Founder dual-signoff per §44.6.4.1 AND paired §M.5 meta-gate enumeration update) is a stronger integrity contract than the family-rooted sibling-split pattern of §21.4.1.B / §21.4.1.D / §21.4.1 row 16 (which use cardinality validators but admit additive expansion of the sibling set via the family-cardinality validator's enumeration). The `not_permitted` override path on the new meta-gate is the §M.4.4.5 row-level `not_permitted` clause that supersedes the default override grammar — closed-set integrity invariants are exactly the use case the override-not-permitted classification was designed for (per the V11 D-11.3-012 row-class amendment). The AE-V72REM-PH4.4-02 row registers the meta-gate authoring separately for §M.5 catalog accountability; the two AE rows ratify jointly at this transition.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — `vendor_page_enrichment_polling` mixed cost-center membership.** The §21.4.6 membership set spans two `billing_mode` values (`customer_billed` for Rows 1 and 2; `sourcera_owned` for Row 3). The Solo throttling rule per §44.6.4 evaluates `surface_throttling_class` regardless of `billing_mode` — the throttling is engine-cost protection (per §44.6.4 / §34.3.5 cost-center routing), not customer-cost protection. Silencing the `sourcera_owned` poller on a Solo Org at envelope exhaustion protects the platform's blended Solo unit economics per §34.3.3 88% margin floor. The cross-reference to §21.4.3 (Platform-Owned Capabilities) is the canonical disambiguation pattern — §21.4.6 is the authoritative storage-of-record for the row; §21.4.3 carries a single-line cross-reference annotation per the v7.2.0-REM Phase 4.4 closure pattern to preserve §21.4.3's platform-owned-capability roster completeness invariant.

**Authored Extension scope clarification — state promotion `alpha → active` without 30-day-in-beta minimum.** Per §4.8.2 state-machine standard path, promotion `beta → active` requires "30 days in beta minimum; rejected-rate <40% sustained over the prior 7 days". The v7.2.0-REM Phase 4.4 closure waives the beta minimum under the v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp-gate exception (mirrors the §21.4.1.B / §21.4.1.C / §21.4.1.E precedent rationale) — the capabilities have been engine-invokable in production since v7.1.0 stamp; the missing registry rows are the structural defect that prevents the §44.6.4 silent-throttling engine from resolving the capability_ids at the §34.8.2 runtime path, not the capability-lifecycle position. AE-V72REM-PH4.4-01 binds the state-machine exception clause; Engineering Lead + Ops Lead dual-signoff at this ratification covers it.

15. **Appendix C v7.2.0-REM Phase 5 block (body_landed_2026-05-20 at `#appendix-c-v72rem-phase-5`)** — 4 new webhook event registrations sequenced by `event_seq` from a single §34.10.5.A invariant #3 5-step atomic transaction: `org.residency_change.initiated` (event_seq=1; webhook + in-app inbox), `org.residency_change.stripe_customer_created` (event_seq=2; webhook only), `org.residency_change.stripe_customer_closed` (event_seq=3; webhook only; `no_op=true` at Org-creation path), `org.residency_change.completed` (event_seq=4; webhook + Loops.so email + in-app inbox). Atomicity guarantee: no partial sequence ever emitted on commit; rollback at any step (i)–(iv) suppresses all four and replaces with `org.residency_change.rollback` (forward-tracked at v7.1.0a stamp). Bridge firewall invariant `console_bridge_no_residency_change_event_kinds` (§M.5 forward-track v7.1.1 stamp) asserts events do NOT cross the Console Bridge. **Self-binding rationale for AE-V72REM-07 early ratification.** AE-V72REM-07 was originally scoped at registration time (2026-05-15) to ratify at the v7.1.1 stamp gate. Early-ratification rationale (v7.1.0a stamp 2026-05-20): the AE *defines* the v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp criteria and release-gate-policy tightening that closes the v7.0.0 release-gate-bypass debt. The v7.1.0a stamp ceremony at 2026-05-20 is the first invocation of the protocol. Deferring AE-V72REM-07 ratification to v7.1.1 would leave the v7.1.0a stamp protocol procedurally floating at the moment it is applied — a self-binding gap. Ratifying at v7.1.0a closes the gap atomically: the protocol is in force the instant it is applied. The release-gate policy preamble amendment landed in this ledger above (the "STATUS (2026-05-20): v7.1.0a hot-patch stamp LANDED" entry) is the codification of the protocol's first-invocation outcome. Engineering Lead counter-signature trigger remains active within 5 BD of named-role hire per AE-V72REM-00.

Reconciliation uses the §34.10.5.A `correction_reason = legacy_uk_ltd_to_eu_gmbh` correction-path entry. This is an **operational deliverable** (Ops Finance ticket queue) not a spec-side deliverable; it carries through under the AE-V72REM-06 Finance Lead counter-signature trigger (5 BD after Finance Lead hire). Until the Finance Lead is staffed, Founder sole-signer (Blake Henry Rowley) holds operational responsibility for the reconciliation pass per AE-V72REM-00.

3. **An owner ratifies a `pending` re-targeted row (e.g., DEF-03 server-side timeout) believing the body still needs to land when in fact it landed in-place at §44.1.** Closed by: DEF-03 and DEF-04 row bodies carry explicit "Verification 2026-05-20: NO supersession by [V8.4/V9 or V3 §6]; body has landed at [§44.1 or §33.6] inline-tagged 'Phase 12.5 R-XX closure'; ratification gesture owed at v7.1.1: Engineering sign-off on the in-place landing (body has landed; only ratification is owed)" annotations. The authoritative Master Spec §M.5.4 catalog-index count is **122 rows** and the §M.5.6 aggregate (§M.5.4 + §M.5.10 V12 + §M.5.12 V13 + §M.5.13 v7.2.0-REM-Phase-1) is **181 gates**; neither is 135. D-11.3-002's Phase-3.2 closure (2026-05-18) made **no gate-row mutation** — §M.5.6 records it as a canonical-row status propagation, and §M.5.6 + the AE-14.18.1-01 row body both state the 13 D-11.3-002 cross-reference orphan rows are **confirmed members of the 122 count, not additive** (the V11 spec-side closure pre-emptively absorbed the anticipated +13). The "135" therefore appears to be a `122 + 13` double-count of orphan rows already inside the 122 — and is the same prompt-task "122 → 135" verbiage already adjudicated against at Phase 3.2. **Resolution:** Master Spec §M.5.4 / §M.5.6 wins per Source-of-Truth Hierarchy §2; AE-14.18.1-01 ratified at 122 / 181, NOT 135. Recommendation to owner: correct the standing "135" expectation in any future Phase-9 prompt restatement.

Both numbers are correct for their respective (nested) scopes. **Critically, this `~135` is a §M.1 surface/engine mapping-row count and is a DIFFERENT artifact from the §M.5 CI-gate-catalog count in conflict #1 — the coincidence of "135" is the likely source of the conflict-#1 confusion.**
